# Applied Thermodynamics: question papers in full

33 unique papers · 2011-12 to 2025-26 · Mechanical

Each paper: year, exam, semester, departments and programmes, then its full text as extracted. Scanned papers were read by OCR, so equations and symbols may contain recognition errors. Structured data: [`questions.jsonl`](questions.jsonl) (one record per question) and [`papers.jsonl`](papers.jsonl) (one record per paper). Index of files: [`README.md`](README.md).

Q2   a. A 30-m high chimney is discharging flue gases at 561 K, when the ambient   [10]
CO-3;   temperature is 294 K. The quantity of air supplied is 18 kg of air per kg of fuel burnt.
SO-1;
Determine (a) draught in mm of water column produced by chimney. (b) equivalent
BL-3
draught in meter of hot gas column. (c) velocity of flue gases in chimney, if 50% of
draught is lost in friction at the grate and passages.
CO-6;   b.   For a diesel engine, brake power is measured as 45 kW when fuel is supplied at a   [10]
SO-1;
rate of 12 kg/h. CV of the fuel is 42 MJ/kg. To cool the engine water is circulated
BL-3
at a rate of 10 kg/minute and the difference in the temperature of water outlet and
inlet is 500C. The exhaust gases pass through a calorimeter, in which water is
circulated at the rate of 10 kg/minute with a rise in its temperature of 350C. Exhaust
gases leave the calorimeter at a temperature of 750C to the ambient at 200C. Specific
heat of water can be taken as 4.2 kJ/kg-K and specific heat of exhaust gases can be
taken as 1.1 kJ/kg-K. Air-fuel ratio is 20. Tabulate the heat balance (kW) for this
engine also calculate IP and mechanical efficiency by considering unaccounted
losses as friction losses.
********** 1 **********
--- page 2 ---
Q3   a. A reheat Rankine cycle receives steam at 3.5 MP a and 350°C to a HP turbine, where   [10]
CO-3; SO-1;   it expands isentropically to 0.8 MPa. The steam is then passed through the boiler
BL-3
and it is reheated to 350°C at 0.8 MPa. The steam is then expanded isentropically to
10 kPa in a LP turbine. Calculate
(a) thermal efficiency, (b) quality of steam leaving the LP turbine, (c) power
developed for a steam flow rate of 6 kg/min.
CO-2; SO-1;   b. Steam enters a convergent nozzle at a pressure of 5 bar and 180 0C and a velocity of   [10]
BL-3   200 m/ s. The discharge pressure is maintained at 3 bar. Determine the required
throat area for a mass flow rate of 0.5 kg/ s and a nozzle efficiency of 96 %.
Q4   a.   Describe functions of cooling towers. Also explain working of surface condenser   [10]
CO-4; SO-1;   with neat sketch.
BL-2
CO-5; SO-1;   b. The condenser and evaporator of a 15 TR ammonia refrigerating plant are   [10]
BL-3   maintained at 25 0C and -10oC. The refrigerant is subcooled by 50C in the condenser.
The vapour leaving the evaporator is 0.97 dry. Calculate the COP and power
required. Use the following properties
Saturation   hf   hfg   sf   sfg
Temperature   (kJ/kg)   (kJ/kg)   (kJ/kg K)   (kJ/kg K)
(o C)

Q5   a. A single-stage, double-acting reciprocating air compressor takes in 15 m3 of air per [10]
CO-1; SO-1;   minute measured at 1.013 bar and 20°C. The delivery pressure is 8 bar and the
BL-3
compressor speed is 400 rpm. The compressor has a clearance volume of 5% of
swept volume with a compression and re-expansion index of n = 1.3. Calculate the
swept volume of the cylinder, the delivery temperature and the indicated power.
CO-3;SO-1;   b   Illustrate and explain the working of Lamont Boiler with neat schematic.   [10]
BL-3
Q6   a. A Single stage impulse turbine is supplied steam at a 4 bar 200oC and it is exhausted [10]
CO-3; SO-1;   at a condenser pressure of 0.15 bar at the rate of 50 kg / min. The steam expands in
BL-3   a nozzle with an efficiency of 90%. The blade speed is 250 m/s and nozzles are
inclined at 20o to the plane of wheel. The blade angle at the exit of moving blade is
30o. Neglecting friction losses in the moving blades, determine
(a) Steam jet velocity
(b) Power developed,
(c) Blade efficiency
(d) Stage efficiency
********** 2 **********
--- page 3 ---
CO-3; SO-1;   b.   Elaborate different aspects of Refrigerant selection. Also illustrate working of   [10]

Q3   a. A steam power plant operates on a theoretical reheat cycle. The steam from   [10]

and find (a) Quality of steam at turbine exhaust, (b) Thermal efficiency of the
cycle, (c) Steam rate in kg/kWh.

overall nozzle efficiency is 94 %. Determine (a) throat, and (b) exit areas.

A reversible heat engine runs between 500°C and 200°C temperature
reservoirs. This heat engine is used to drive an auxiliary and a reversible

Figures on the right hand side indicate full marks.

Use of steam table allowed.

Answer any five of the following:

a) State and explain the different types of thermodynamic system.

b) Define point function and path function with example.

c) State the First law of thermodynamics for closed system undergoing cycle and process.

d) Define i) Reversible and irreversible process

ii) Intensive and Extensive properties

e) What is quasi-static process? State its significance

f) Show that internal energy is the property of a system.

g) State and explain the principle of increase of entropy

h) What is cut-off ratio? How does it effect the thermal efficiency of Diesel cycle.

i) State the assumption made in the air-standard cycle.

j) Draw P-V and T-S diagram for Otto and Dual cycle.

a) Prove that the heat absorbed or rejected during a polytropic process for ideal gas
is given by [(y-n)/(y-1)] x work done.

b) A certain gas occupies a volume of 0.3m? at a pressure of 0.2 bar. The temperature of the

‘ gas at this state is 350 K. The gas undergoes a constant volume of process until the

| ) pressure raises to 7 bar. Determine the temperature at the end of process, change in

internal energy, change in enthalpy and change in entropy during the process. Take
Cv=0.712 kj/kgk R=0.287 kj/kgk

c) A certain mass of air initially at a pressure of 480 KPa and a temperature of 190°C is
expanded adiabatically to a pressure of 94 KPa. It is then heated at constant volume until

it attain initial temperature when the pressure is found to be 150 KPa. State the ‘type of
Determine a) The index of adiabatic expansion b) W.D/Kg c) Change in specific entropy

State the limitation of first law of thermodynamics and explain the second law of

thermodynamics.

One Kg of ice at -20°C is exposed to the atmosphere which is at 20°C .The ice melts and

comes in to thermal equilibrium with the atmosphere. Calculate the entropy increase of

the universe. Take Cp of ice as 2.093 kj/kgk and latent heat of the fusion of ice as 334.5

kj/kg.

A reversible heat engine operates between two reservoirs at 600°C and 40°C. The engine

drives a reversible refrigerator which operates between same 40°C reservoir and a

reservoir at -18°C.The heat transfer to the heat engine is 2100 kJ and there is net work

output of 370 kJ from the combined plant. Evaluate the heat transfer to the refrigerator and

net heat transfer to the 40°C reservoir.

Derive steady flow energy equation. State the assumption made.

A centrifugal pump delivers the 50 kg of water per second .The inlet and outlet pressures

are 1 bar and 4.2 bar respectively .The suction is 2.2 m below the centre of pump and

delivery is 8.5 m above the centre of the pump. The suction and delivery pipe diameter are

20 cm and 10 cm respectively. Determine the capacity of the electric motor to run the

pump.

In a gas turbine the gas enters at the rate of 5 kg/s with a velocity of 50 m/s and enthalpy

of 900 kj/kg and leaves the turbine with a velocity of 150 m/s and enthalpy 400 kj/kg. The

loss of heat from the gases to the surrounding is 25 kj/kg. Assume for gas R=0.285 kj/kgk

and Cp=1.004 kj/kgk and inlet conditions to be at 100 Kpa and 27°C.Determine the power

output of the turbine and diameter of the inlet pipe.

Derive the expression for efficiency of Diesel cycle.

i) An engine working on the Otto cycle is supplied with air at 0.1MPa, 35°. The

compression ratio is 8. Heat supplied is 2100 KJ/Kg. Calculate the maximum pressure and

maximum temperature of the cycle,cycle efficiency and the mean effective pressure.(For

air Cp=1.005KJ/KgK cy =0.718 KJ/Kg K)

y A gas turbine plant operates on the Brayton Cycle between Tmin=300K and
Tmax=1073K. Find the maximum work done per kg of air and the corresponding cycle

efficiency. How does this efficiency compare with the Carnot cycle efficiency operating

between the same two temperatures?

Explain ‘Equation of State’.

Explain Moiler Diagram.

Steam at 20bar,360°c is expanded in a’ steam turbine to 0.08 bar. It then enters a

condenser,where it is condensed to saturated liquid water.The pump feeds back the water

into the boiler.

i)Assuming ideal processes, find per kg of steam the net work and the cycle efficiency.

ii)If the turbine and the pump have each 80% efficiency, find the percentage reduction in

* the net work and the cycle efficiency.

b)

} What is quality of steam? Explain any one method for measurement of steam quality?
iSteann initially atl. SMPa, 300% expands reversibly and adiabatically in a steam turbine
to 40°c. Determine the ideal work output of turbine per kg of steam.
ii)Explain Dalton’s law of partial pressure.

6. Figures in brackets on the right hand side indicate full marks.

Q.1) Attempt any four questions from the following. , , ‘ (4X5)

A) Explain Zeroth law of thermodynamics and state it’s significance.

B) 85 kJ of heat is supplied to system at a constant volume. The system rejects 90 KJ of heat’at constant
pressure and 20 KJ of work is done on it. The system is brought to it’s original state by adiabatic
process. Determine values of internal energy at all end states if it’s initial value is 100 KJ.

C): Write down Steady flow energy equation based on energy balance basis. Also apply the same to-

(1) Water Turbine (2) Boiler
D) Write a note on “Virial equation of state”.
E) Find the enthalpy, entropy and internal energy for 5 Kg of steam at 14 bar and 300°C.

Q.2) Attempt following questions (29)
A)I0.5 Kg of air is compressed reversibly.and adiabatically from 80 KPa, 60°C to 0.4 MPa, and is then
expanded at constant pressure to the original volume. Sketch the process on p-v and T-s plane.
Calculate heat transfer and work transfer for the whole path. (10)
B) Write down a note on carnot cycle. Also derive an expression for the efficiency of the same. (10)

Q.3) Attempt following questions (20)
A) Ina gas turbine the gas enters at the rate of 5 Kg/sec witha velocity of 50 m/sec and enthalpy of
900 KJ/Kg and leaves the turbine with a velocity of 150 m/sec and enthalpy of 400 KJ/Kg. The loss
of heat from the gases to the surroundings is 25 KJ/Kg. Assume for gas R= 0.285 KJ/Kg-K and Cp =
v1. ge J/Kg-K and the inlet conditions to be at 100 KPa and 27°C.

Determine the power output of the turbine and the diameter of the inlet pipe. (08)
B) Explain phase change diagram of water on p-v-T surface. (08)
C) Write down the sign conventions for heat and work transfer across the boundary. (04)
Q.4) Attempt following questions (20) ~
A) Represerit Otto‘cycle on p-V and T-s plane. Also derive an expression for the efficiency of the
same. (08)

a).
--- page 2 ---
_ B) Solve any two questions from the following Ries.
1) Write a note on ‘Throttling Calorimeter’. .
2) What do you mean by property of a system? Show that energy is a property of the system.
3) Write a note on brayton cycle.

Q.5) Attempt following questions. (20)
A) An engine working on the dual cycle has a cylinder bore of 20cm and stroke of 40cm. The
compression ratio is 14.5 and the pressure ratio of the constant volume heat addition process is 1.5.
The constant pressure heat addition cut off takes place at 4.9 percent of the stroke. Determine the air

standard efficiency. Assume Y=14 i (10)
B) State and prove “Carnot theorem” (10)
Q.6) Attempt any five questions from the following (4X5)

A) Write down difference between Closed and Open system.

B) Define-(1) Specific heat (2) Sublimation (3) Saturation pressure (4) Subcooled liquid
C) Write dow difference between closed system and adiabatic system.

D) Derive an equation for workdone during polytropic process.

E) Prove that slope of constant volume line is more than constant pressure line on T-s plane:
F) Explain- PMM-I and PMM-II

Q.1 Answer any five of the following. 20
a) State the Zeroth law of thermodynamics. What is its significance?
b) Define point function and path function with example
c) Define i) Reversible and irreversible process
ii) Intensive and Extensive properties
d) What is quasi-static process? State its significance.
f) Show (COP)yp = 1 + (COP)g
g) State and explain the principle of increase of entropy
i) What is cut-off ratio? How does it effect the thermal efficiency of Diesel cycle?

{ b) One kg of nitrogen at a temperature of 150°C occupies a volume of 0.2 m°.The gas 08
; undergoes fully restricted constant-pressure expansion without friction to a final
volume of 0.36 m?.Calculate the final temperature, work done, heat transferred and
change in entropy. Take Cp= 0.743 KJ/KgK, R=0.297 KJ/KgK
4 c) In a gas turbine the gas enters at the rate of 5 kg/s with a velocity of 50 m/s and 08
} enthalpy of 900 KJ/Kg and leaves the turbine with a velocity of 150 m/s and enthalpy
400 KJ/Kg. The loss of heat from the gases to the surrounding is 25 KJ/Kg..The inlet
condition to be 100 KPa and 27°C.Determine the power output of the turbine and
diameter of the pipe Assume for gas R= 0.285 KJ/KgK and Cp = 1.004 KJ/KgK
Q.3 a) State Kelvin- plank and clausis statement of second law of thermodynamics. Also - 07

b) A heat pump is used to maintain an auditorium at 25°C, when the atmospheric 07
temperature is 10 °C.The heat load of the hall is 1500 KJ/min.Calculate the power
required to run the heat pump. If its COP is 30 % of the carnot heat pump working
between the same temperatures. >
. c) ASO kg of block iron casting at 500 K is thrown in to large lake which is at a 06
q ) temperature of 285 K .After the iron block reaches thermal equilibrium with the lake

a) Entropy change for iron block
b) Entropy change of the lake water

300 Kg of fish is to be frozen at -2 °C.The specific heat of fish above freezing point is
4.187 KJ/KgK and the latent heat of fusion is 234.5 KJ/Kg..The freezing point is -2
°C.A refrigerator is used for this purpose which rejects the heat in ambient at 40
°C.The COP of the refrigerator is 60 % of COP of the carnot refrigerator operating
between between the same temperature limit. How much power must be required to
remove the heat in 10 hrs?

Compare Otto, Diesel and Dual cycle for same compression ratio and same heat
supply with the help of P-V and T-S diagram.

The compression and expansion ratio of an oil engine working on air standard dual cycle are
9 and 5 respectively. The initial temperature and pressure are 1 bar and 30 °C respectively.
The heat librated at constant pressure is twice the heat librated at constant volume.
